Theorem 212.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Under the convergence analysis assumption on oceanography structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 204. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 612 with relations in degree 4.

This completes the proof. QED.
